Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The mechanisms variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 287 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 394 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by mechanisms generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 287, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 394. QED.
